1. Executive Summary & Architectural Overview

In this release of Apple Silicon iBoot (fac00ca.iBoot.v63.RESEARCH_RELEASE.bin), operating system loading and execution handover are divided across a two-tier execution boundary between EL0 (Task Context) and EL1 (Kernel/Supervisor Context):

  1. OS Ingestion, Parsing, and Staging (EL0 Task Mode):
    • High-level boot commands (do_bootx at 0xFFFFFC01FC0D1284, do_memboot at 0xFFFFFC01FC0D19AC) invoke the unified loading coordinator sub_FFFFFC01FC0D109C.
    • The kernelcache Mach-O binary is located (via NVRAM/disk paths like /System/Library/Caches/com.apple.kernelcaches/kernelcache or staging buffers) and validated by sub_FFFFFC01FC0F9908 (load_kernelcache).
    • Image segmentation and physical memory layout are performed by sub_FFFFFC01FC0FA2E0 (lay_out_opaque_kernelcache) and sub_FFFFFC01FC0F6114 (load-kernel-start), resolving physical mappings and preparing the boot_args structure.
  2. EL0 Quiescing and Syscall Trampoline:
    • sub_FFFFFC01FC162F18 coordinates driver teardown, bus quiescing, display/USB shutdown, and terminal serial output flush ("======== End of %s serial output. ========" at 0xFFFFFC01FC163024).
    • sub_FFFFFC01FC1630F8 executes a software interrupt via SVC 3 (0xFFFFFC01FC163108), passing physical pointers to boot_args and configuration descriptors into EL1.
  3. EL1 Dispatch, Hardware Lockdown, and Execution Handover:
    • The exception is trapped at vector entry exception_vectors_el1 + 0x400 (0xFFFFFC01FC2F1C00), decoded via ESR_EL1 (EC = 0x15), and routed through el1_syscall_dispatch (0xFFFFFC01FC2FC284) jump table case 3 (0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5F0) to sub_FFFFFC01FC300174.
    • sub_FFFFFC01FC300174 terminates remaining EL0 tasks and unmaps task address space, invoking the terminal boot driver sub_FFFFFC01FC162E14.
    • sub_FFFFFC01FC162E14 fetches the 64-bit kernel entry point (0x10074000000) via sub_FFFFFC01FC306410 / sub_FFFFFC01FC108D58, applies ARMv8.3-A Pointer Authentication (PACIA1716 with modifier 0), locks Configurable Text Read-only Region (CTRR) via MSR S3_0_C11_C9_0, X0 (sub_FFFFFC01FC104A50), shuts down clock/power domains (sub_FFFFFC01FC116954), and performs the definitive handover via BLRAAZ X24 at 0xFFFFFC01FC162EDC.

2. Assumption Register & Stress-Test Matrix

IDAssumptionGrounded EvidenceStress Test / Falsification ProbeDependent Findings
A1Handover uses ARMv8.3-A PAC with Key IA and zero modifierDisassembly at 0xFFFFFC01FC162E98 (MOV X16, #0), 0xFFFFFC01FC162E9C (PACIA1716), and 0xFFFFFC01FC162EDC (BLRAAZ X24).Probe whether CRn=C2, CRm=C1 (APIAKey) is re-keyed prior to instruction execution. If APIAKey is zeroed, PAC reduces to a standard branch.Handover instruction authenticity; kernel entry vector expectation.
A2Handover occurs at EL1Vector table exception_vectors_el1 set in MSR VBAR_EL1, X10 at 0xFFFFFC01FC0D019C; vector offset +0x400 handles lower EL (EL0).Falsified if HCR_EL2 traps EL1 exceptions into EL2 or if hyp mode is active. (At 0xFFFFFC01FC0D0000, EL2 initialises EL1 and branches directly).Target operating system execution privilege level (Darwin kernel runs at EL1).

3. Step-by-Step Functional Flow

[ CLI / Boot Engine ]
   │
   ├──> do_bootx (0xFFFFFC01FC0D1284) / do_memboot (0xFFFFFC01FC0D19AC)
   │       │
   │       └──> sub_FFFFFC01FC0D109C (High-Level Boot Orchestrator)
   │               │
   │               ├──> sub_FFFFFC01FC0FD408 -> sub_FFFFFC01FC0FA2E0 (lay_out_opaque_kernelcache)
   │               │       ├── Validates Mach-O headers (sub_FFFFFC01FC0F9908)
   │               │       ├── Copies Mach-O segments to target physical pages
   │               │       └── Formats devicetree / boot_args (returned via &v54, &v53)
   │               │
   │               └──> sub_FFFFFC01FC162F18 (EL0 Pre-Handover Shutdown)
   │                       ├── Prints "======== End of %s serial output. ========" (0xFFFFFC01FC163024)
   │                       ├── Flushes caches, serial FIFOs, and PCIe/USB controllers
   │                       │
   │                       └──> sub_FFFFFC01FC1630F8 (Syscall Trampoline)
   │                               └──> SVC 3 (0xFFFFFC01FC163108)
   │                                       │
   │  ┌────────────────────────────────────┘
   │  ▼
[ ARM64 Exception Vector (EL1) ]
   │
   ├──> exception_vectors_el1 + 0x400 (0xFFFFFC01FC2F1C00 - Lower EL Synchronous Exception)
   │       │
   │       ├── Reads ESR_EL1, checks EC == 0x15 (SVC instruction)
   │       └── B loc_FFFFFC01FC2FC284 (el1_syscall_dispatch)
   │               │
   │               └──> Switch Case 3 (0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5F0)
   │                       │
   │                       └──> sub_FFFFFC01FC300174 (EL1 Environment Teardown)
   │                               ├── Terminates EL0 user task structures
   │                               ├── Invalidates EL0 translation regime (TLBI)
   │                               │
   │                               └──> sub_FFFFFC01FC162E14 (Final Handover Executor)
   │                                       ├── sub_FFFFFC01FC306410 -> sub_FFFFFC01FC108D58 (Entry Point = 0x10074000000)
   │                                       ├── PACIA1716 (Signs entry point -> X24)
   │                                       ├── sub_FFFFFC01FC104A50 (MSR S3_0_C11_C9_0, CTRR hardware lock)
   │                                       ├── sub_FFFFFC01FC116954 (SoC clock/power final quiesce)
   │                                       ├── Prepares registers: X0=boot_args, X1..X4
   │                                       │
   │                                       └──> 0xFFFFFC01FC162EDC: BLRAAZ X24  ===> JUMP TO KERNEL
   │                                               │
   │                                               └──> 0xFFFFFC01FC0D04C4 (Trap / Panic if kernel returns)

4. Code Breakdown & Concrete Disassembly

4.1. OS Image Staging & Parsing: sub_FFFFFC01FC0F9908 & sub_FFFFFC01FC0FA2E0

  • Location: 0xFFFFFC01FC0F9908 (load_kernelcache) and 0xFFFFFC01FC0FA2E0 (lay_out_opaque_kernelcache).
  • Functionality:
    • Validates Mach-O magic (0xFEEDFACF / MH_MAGIC_64).
    • Iterates over LC_SEGMENT_64 commands (__TEXT, __DATA, __KLD, __PRELINK_TEXT, __PRELINK_DATA).
    • Implements the opaque layout algorithm (evidenced by format strings In lay_out_opaque_kernelcache 1st source at 0xFFFFFC01FC57FC29 and 1st dest at 0xFFFFFC01FC57FC52).
    • Copies Mach-O memory chunks to physical RAM apertures while allocating physical pages for devicetree, trust cache, and boot_args.

4.2. EL0 Execution Quiescing: sub_FFFFFC01FC162F18

  • Location: 0xFFFFFC01FC162F18 - 0xFFFFFC01FC1630F4 (Length: 476 bytes, 119 instructions).
  • Functionality:
    • Invokes platform reset callbacks for peripherals (sub_FFFFFC01FC26FCF4, sub_FFFFFC01FC3152F8).
    • Shuts down hardware workloops and telemetry breadcrumbs (ADRL X0, "boot-breadcrumbs" at 0xFFFFFC01FC162F74).
    • Prints terminal serial message:
0xFFFFFC01FC163024 ADRL X0, aEndOfSSerialOu ; "======== End of %s serial output. ========\n"
0xFFFFFC01FC16302C BL   printf_dbg
  • Flushes UART buffers and calls trampoline:
0xFFFFFC01FC1630D0 MOV  X0, X20    ; Physical address of boot_args structure
0xFFFFFC01FC1630D4 MOV  X1, X19    ; Physical address of devicetree / auxiliary data
0xFFFFFC01FC1630D8 MOV  X2, X21    ; Subsystem mode (3 = OS Handover)
0xFFFFFC01FC1630DC BL   sub_FFFFFC01FC1630F8

4.3. Exception Level Transition: sub_FFFFFC01FC1630F8

  • Location: 0xFFFFFC01FC1630F8 - 0xFFFFFC01FC163124.
  • Functionality:
    • Sets up immediate arguments and drops from EL0 to EL1:
0xFFFFFC01FC1630F8 PACIBSP
0xFFFFFC01FC1630FC STP  X29, X30, [SP,#-0x10]!
0xFFFFFC01FC163100 MOV  X29, SP
0xFFFFFC01FC163104 MOV  W3, #1
0xFFFFFC01FC163108 SVC  3                   ; <--- System Call 3: Transition to EL1 Kernel Handover
0xFFFFFC01FC16310C MOV  X0, #0x6476B5993BF0843 ; (Hash / file ID)
0xFFFFFC01FC16311C MOV  W1, #0x69           ; Line 105
0xFFFFFC01FC163120 BL   panic_lineno        ; Non-returning fallback if SVC returns

4.4. EL1 Syscall Dispatcher: el1_syscall_dispatch & sub_FFFFFC01FC300174

  • Location:
    • Vector handler: 0xFFFFFC01FC2F1C00 (exception_vectors_el1 + 0x400).
    • Dispatcher: 0xFFFFFC01FC2FC284 (el1_syscall_dispatch).
    • Syscall 3 branch: 0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5F0.
  • Functionality:
    • 0xFFFFFC01FC2F1C3C: Extracts Exception Syndrome Register MRS X20, ESR_EL1.
    • 0xFFFFFC01FC2F1C40: Verifies Exception Class LSR W21, W20, #0x1A == 0x15 (EC_AA64_SVC).
    • 0xFFFFFC01FC2FC284: Decodes syscall index AND W7, W20, #0xFFFF (Index = 3).
    • Evaluates jumptable jpt_FFFFFC01FC2FC37C (0xFFFFFC01FC2FFDA8), branching to offset 0x27C (0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5F0):
0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5F0 CMP  X26, #0         ; Validates X3 parameter
0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5F4 CSET W3, NE
0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5F8 MOV  X0, X23         ; Argument 0 (boot_args)
0xFFFFFC01FC2FC5FC MOV  X1, X22         ; Argument 1
0xFFFFFC01FC2FC600 MOV  X2, X25         ; Argument 2
0xFFFFFC01FC2FC604 BL   sub_FFFFFC01FC300174 ; Core teardown & handover invocation
  • In sub_FFFFFC01FC300174:
    • Dismantles EL0 tasks, flushes memory management state (TLBI VMALLE1), releases locks, and invokes terminal function sub_FFFFFC01FC162E14 (0xFFFFFC01FC3005FC).

4.5. Definitive Handover Routine: sub_FFFFFC01FC162E14

  • Location: 0xFFFFFC01FC162E14 - 0xFFFFFC01FC162F18 (Length: 260 bytes, 65 instructions).
  • Disassembly Analysis:
0xFFFFFC01FC162E14 PACIBSP
0xFFFFFC01FC162E18 SUB    SP, SP, #0x60
...
; Retrieve kernel entry point address
0xFFFFFC01FC162E90 BL     sub_FFFFFC01FC306410 ; Invokes sub_FFFFFC01FC108D58: returns X0 = 0x10074000000
0xFFFFFC01FC162E94 MOV    X17, X0             ; X17 = 0x0000010074000000 (Target Entry Point)
0xFFFFFC01FC162E98 MOV    X16, #0             ; X16 = 0 (PAC Modifier)
0xFFFFFC01FC162E9C PACIA1716                  ; Authenticate pointer: signs X17 with Key IA, Mod 0 -> X17
0xFFFFFC01FC162EA0 MOV    X24, X17            ; X24 = PAC-signed Entry Point

; Hardware Security & CTRR Locking
0xFFFFFC01FC162EA4 BL     sub_FFFFFC01FC104AB4
0xFFFFFC01FC162EA8 SUB    X8, X23, #3
0xFFFFFC01FC162EAC CMP    X8, #1
0xFFFFFC01FC162EB0 B.HI   loc_FFFFFC01FC162EC0
0xFFFFFC01FC162EB4 BL     sub_FFFFFC01FC104A50 ; Locks CTRR: writes MSR S3_0_C11_C9_0, X0
0xFFFFFC01FC162EB8 BL     sub_FFFFFC01FC16503C
0xFFFFFC01FC162EBC BL     sub_FFFFFC01FC116954 ; Final SoC clock / power tree quiesce

; Setup Kernel Calling Convention Registers
loc_FFFFFC01FC162EC0:
0xFFFFFC01FC162EC0 CBZ    X24, loc_FFFFFC01FC162F00
0xFFFFFC01FC162EC4 BL     sub_FFFFFC01FC309214 ; Reads memory configuration parameter
0xFFFFFC01FC162EC8 MOV    X4, X0              ; X4 = auxiliary handover payload
0xFFFFFC01FC162ECC MOV    X0, X22             ; X0 = Physical address of boot_args structure
0xFFFFFC01FC162ED0 MOV    X1, X21             ; X1 = Secondary argument
0xFFFFFC01FC162ED4 MOV    X2, X20             ; X2 = Tertiary argument
0xFFFFFC01FC162ED8 MOV    X3, X19             ; X3 = Quaternary argument

; Execution Handover
0xFFFFFC01FC162EDC BLRAAZ X24                 ; <=== EXECUTION HANDOVER: Branch with link to authenticated X24
0xFFFFFC01FC162EE0 BL     sub_FFFFFC01FC0D04C4 ; If kernel returns: HINT #0x45; infinite halt loop

5. Architectural Parameters & Register State at Handover

RegisterArchitectural RoleValue / Source
PC TargetKernel Entry Point0x10074000000 (authenticated via PACIA1716 using Key IA, Modifier = 0).
X0boot_args pointerPhysical base address of the formatted boot_args structure (containing memory map, console config, device tree physical address, and kernel command-line arguments).
X1Video / Display ContextStaged frame buffer parameters or devicetree secondary physical pointer.
X2Staging / ConfigurationConfigured subsystem mode flag (propagated from SVC 3 dispatch).
X3Reserved / CPU flagsSecondary boot configuration flags.
X4Memory DescriptorPointer retrieved from global memory descriptor sub_FFFFFC01FC309214 (MEMORY[0x3F000039FC0]).
X16 / X17PAC Authentication RegistersScratchpad registers consumed during pointer authentication (X16 = 0, X17 = PAC-tagged address).
X24Authenticated Branch TargetCarries the PAC-tagged pointer consumed by BLRAAZ X24.
SP_EL1Stack PointerMapped to the temporary EL1 boot stack.
S3_0_C11_C9_0Apple CTRR Lock RegisterProgrammed with BIT(63) set (0x8000000000000000) via sub_FFFFFC01FC104A50 to make kernel text memory hardware-immutable.

6. Bounded Scope Expansion (Unconventional Opportunities & Risks)

CategoryFinding / PatternImpactTechnical Grounding & Architectural Risk
Security ArchitectureHardware-Enforced CTRR Lock (MSR S3_0_C11_C9_0)HighAt 0xFFFFFC01FC162EB4, iBoot writes to Apple-specific system register S3_0_C11_C9_0 before jumping to the kernel. This permanently locks CTRR (Configurable Text Read-only Region), preventing both EL1 and EL0 from altering or executing writable kernel memory regions regardless of MMU page table modifications.
Reliability / Fault HandlingPost-Handover Infinite Halt Trap (sub_FFFFFC01FC0D04C4)MediumIf the operating system kernel returns from its entry point (RET), control falls through to 0xFFFFFC01FC162EE0 calling sub_FFFFFC01FC0D04C4. This routine executes HINT #0x45 followed by an unconditional infinite spin (while(1)), causing a hardware watchdog reset rather than attempting an uncoordinated fallback to iBoot.
Exploitation / Attack SurfaceSVC 3 Argument Descriptors in EL0LowThe physical address arguments passed to SVC 3 originate in EL0 task memory (v54, v53). However, sub_FFFFFC01FC300174 enforces strict sanity bounds checks (CMP X8, X9, bounds_fail_ptr_oob at 0xFFFFFC01FC300600) before copying the descriptors into EL1-managed memory and setting up CTRR registers.
